# run log: docs/videos/claude-skill-manual-event-folder, brief.md "要先實作" items M0 to M7, the L group

Date: 2026-10-21 Taipei / 2026-10-21 UTC (every item carries its own UTC time; first item 00:23:36Z,
    last planned session ended 00:50:37Z; the added W2 pair ran 01:33:18Z–01:35:03Z). All sessions ran the same
    day, on the same Claude Code version.
Claude Code: 2.1.295 (Claude Code)
Node.js: v24.13.0
bash: GNU bash, version 5.3.15(1)-release (x86_64-pc-cygwin)   (Git Bash)
OS: `uname -s -r -m`: MINGW64_NT-10.0-26200-ARM64 3.6.9-b4195d69.x86_64 x86_64 (Windows 11 Home 10.0.26200)
Model: every session goes through <work>/_tools/runs.sh -> session.sh, which passes `--model sonnet`. All
    29 sessions reported model "claude-sonnet-5-5" on their init line and in modelUsage.

How to read this file
- Everything between the ===== bars is quoted from files the commands wrote: <work>/runner/M*.txt for the
  M items (the runner ran each command and wrote "$ command", its output and "[exit N]") and, for every
  session, the output of `bash "$T/runs.sh" <id>`, which is the whole <work>/run/logs/<id>.session.log
  (session.sh's part, then runs.sh's skillview / mine / cover-check part). The only edits: the home
  directory is written <home>, the video work area <work>, the repository <repo>. session.sh already writes
  <folder> for the session folder in the parts it filters; tool calls in the stream summary keep the full
  path, masked to <work>\run\lab\講座籌備. Long tool inputs are cut at 300 characters by inspect.mjs itself.
- Lines starting "NOTE (runner):" are the runner's own remarks. They are not output.
- "[exit N]" after a command is that command's exit code. Inside a session log, "[exit N]  stderr bytes: M"
  is the claude process's exit code and the size of its stderr.
- <work> is <home>/mokaair-work/videos/claude-skill-manual-event-folder (outside the repository).
  $T = <work>/_tools, $E = $T/seed/extras. Sessions ran in <work>/run/lab/講座籌備, rebuilt from the seed
  for every session; what each left is in <work>/run/snap/<id>/. Raw stream-json files stay in
  <work>/run/logs and are not in the repository (they carry the cwd and session ids).
- A session counts when it exits 0 with 0 bytes of stderr and a sonnet init line. All 29 counted; no
  timeout; no spare name (an "r" id) was used.
- What the cards can show is copied to docs/videos/claude-skill-manual-event-folder/demo/ (see the last
  section).
- Where the commands were started from: a Git Bash shell that is itself a child of a Claude Code desktop
  session (the same as in the first video's run log). It carries environment variables whose names start
  with CLAUDE or ANTHROPIC; sessions inherit them. A viewer's own terminal would not have these.
- "first=" is the input side (input + cache write + cache read tokens) of the session's first model
  request; "growth" is the step from the request before the booklet arrived to the first one after it;
  "requests carrying it" counts the requests from that one to the end (skillview.mjs's own definitions,
  printed at the top of that script).

- **名單累計 18 人**：素食類 5 位、海鮮過敏 2 位（周家豪、黃雅婷）、其他忌口 2 位（王志明不吃牛、鄭宇翔不吃辣）。

Skill 會涵蓋這幾件事:

- **不處理的:**
  - 測試資料不進任何名單。
  - 備註寫取消的不寄信,已在名單裡的會移除並寫進日誌。
- **重複報名:**
  - 同一個 Email 以最晚填的那筆為準。
  - 先沒填 Email、之後補上的,合併成一筆。
  - 同名但 Email 和部門都不同的(例如兩個王志明)視為不同人,並在日誌提醒你確認。
- **分流:**
  - 沒有 Email 或格式不對的,放進 `要補資料.csv`。
  - 飲食需求空白不算缺資料。
  - 之前缺資料、這次補齊的,移進確認信名單。
- **格式:** 部門統一成「○○部」,Email 轉小寫並去掉空白。
- **處理日誌:** 在 `處理日誌.md` 結尾追加一段新批次的紀錄,內容包含:
  - 新增人數與累計人數。
  - 素食和過敏人數,連還缺 Email 的人也算,因為備餐要用。
  - 需要人工確認的事項,例如有人問能不能改線上參加(不算取消)、人數超過 30 人名額。
- **範圍:** 只動 `輸出/` 底下三個檔案,不寄信,也不去聯絡要補資料的人。

有兩點是我從第一批的結果推出來的,不是你明講的規則,可以再看一下:

- **確認信名單的排序:** 我推出來的規則是「依各人第一次有有效 Email 的那筆報名排序」,所以補上 Email 的林佳蓉排在最後。
- **素食人數的算法:** 我推出來的規則是包含還缺 Email 的人,第一批的日誌「素食類 5 位」要把缺 Email 的劉思妤算進去才對得上。

====================================================================================================
What is in demo/ (docs/videos/claude-skill-manual-event-folder/demo/)
====================================================================================================
- booklets/book-lean, book-fat, book-vague, book-mine: each booklet's SKILL.md and appendix pages as the
  sessions got them (book-fat has no appendix files: they are inside its SKILL.md).
- shelf5/: the five other booklets' SKILL.md (speaker-intro, meeting-notes, expense-receipts, poster-copy,
  reminder-mail).
- claude-md/: claude-md-book.CLAUDE.md (used in LM and CM), claude-md-specific / claude-md-vague (the first
  video's, in the seed but not used here), appendix-loose/ (the two pages loose in the folder for LM, CP, CM).
- prompts/: time.txt, skill-auto.txt, paste.txt, write-skill.txt (and skill-slash.txt, not used here).
- pack/: the M4 zip listings as text (signup-check.zip.txt, signup-check-wrong.zip.txt).
- start/: the second batch (報名表_第二批.csv) and the round1 輸出/ every session started from.
- <id>/ for each of the 29 sessions (27 planned + W2a, W2b): reply.md (the final reply), skillview.txt (the skillview summary lines,
  and mine.mjs / cover-check.mjs where runs.sh adds them), files-after.txt (hash, mtime, path after the run);
  the C, W1, W2 and G1 sessions also have 輸出/ as the session left it (W1a/W1b and W2a/W2b include 待回覆.csv). L sessions
  changed no files, so they have no 輸出/. G1/attempted-SKILL.md is the text of G1's refused Write.
- No raw stream-json, no debug logs, no session ids, no personal paths.
